===== 8. PROPERTY SERVER MODEL / PRIVACY / UPGRADES =====
1-"""
2-RealmLife server-authoritative property + household system.
3-
4-Core rules
5-----------
6-- Every RealmLife account has exactly one housing position:
7-    * original owner of one property
8-      OR
9-    * member of another household.
10:- Every property and yard is private by default.
11-- Guests enter only by owner/household invitation or approved request.
12-- Guest authorization ends when that guest leaves the property.
13-- Household members may use/upgrade the shared property.
14-- Only the ORIGINAL property owner may destroy the property.
15-- Every property Fire Power contribution records the contributor.
16-- Property destruction returns floor(contributor_total / 2)
17-  to EACH contributor separately.
18-- Joining another household surrenders the joining owner's previous
19-  personal property and refunds that property's contributors at 50%.
20-"""
